## Document 017: 2000年历史重演，反弹之后才是真正的崩跌？

Source PDF: all report pdf/2000年历史重演，反弹之后才是真正的崩跌？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

TS Lombard 警告，当前科技股抛售向⼤盘蔓延的⾛势，与 2000 年互联⽹泡沫破裂前⼣惊
⼈相似：科技股先跌、市场反弹，随后危机进⼀步扩⼤。尽管当前科技巨头盈利和基本⾯
更强，估值泡沫也已部分消化，但 AI 投资热潮的乐观叙事与当年如出⼀辙，投资者不应低
估反弹后再度⼤跌的⻛险。
摘要：
此轮科技股抛售正在向更⼴泛市场蔓延，令部分分析师想起了互联⽹泡沫破裂前⼣的市场形
态。
伦敦经济研究机构 TS Lombard 的分析显⽰，当前市场⾛势与 2000 年互联⽹泡沫崩溃前的路径存
在令⼈不安的相似之处 —— 科技股率先下跌，⼤盘随后跟进，⽽历史上真正的崩跌恰恰发⽣在
⼀轮显著反弹之后。
该机构宏观策略师 Dario Perkins 警告称， "与 2000 年夏天、崩盘前⼣存在⼀些令⼈忧虑的相似之
处。 "
⽬前，标普 500 指数正⾯临 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最差单周表现，此前数周抛售主要集中于科技板块，
如今已明显向更⼴泛市场扩散。与此同时，科技板块在标普 500 指数中的权重已超过 40% ，较

2000 年峰值的 35% 更⾼，若将 Meta Platforms 和 Alphabet 等被归⼊通信服务板块的科技巨头⼀
并计⼊，这⼀⽐例上⽉更突破 46% ，意味着科技板块对整体市场的潜在冲击⼒远超当年。
历史镜鉴：反弹掩盖了真正的转折
据彭博报道， TS Lombard 的分析还原了 2000 年的市场演变路径：纳斯达克综合指数于当年 3 ⽉
10 ⽇触顶，随后⾄ 4 ⽉中旬跌逾三分之⼀，但此后⾄ 6 ⽉中旬⼜反弹 35%—— 投资者趁低买⼊，
并将资⾦转向盈利能⼒较强的⼤型科技公司。
彼时，距纳斯达克⻅顶已过去六个⽉，标普 500 指数仍处于历史⾼位。
然⽽，真正的破坏随后才开始显现。
较弱科技企业的融资环境收紧、信⽤利差扩⼤、盈利预期下调，开始拖累那些以科技公司为客
⼾的盈利型企业。纳斯达克在此后三个⽉内再度暴跌逾 40% 。Perkins 指出， " 瓶颈并不是看多的
理由 —— 它们可能预⽰着⼀场⼤规模的供给过剩。 " 他补充说，当时看似健康的订单簿掩盖了即
将到来的转折。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
当前⾛势：抛售从科技蔓延⾄⼤盘
此轮下跌的导⽕索，是市场对⼈⼯智能过度投资的担忧，以及⺠主党横扫国会后的政策不确定
性。纳斯达克⾃ 10 ⽉初的历史⾼点回落后持续承压，标普 500 起初表现相对抗跌，但如今已难以
独善其⾝。
科技板块在指数中的超⾼权重，放⼤了其对整体市场的传导⻛险。信息技术板块权重上⽉已超
过标普 500 的 40% ，超越 2000 年 35% 的历史峰值；若将 Meta Platforms 和 Alphabet 纳⼊统计，⼴
义科技板块的权重上⽉累计超过 46% 。
这意味着，⼀旦科技板块持续承压，对⼤盘的拖累效应将远⽐ 26 年前更为显著。
今昔差异：估值泡沫已提前消化
当然， 2026 年与 2000 年之间也存在重要差异。
2000 年互联⽹泡沫⾼峰期，⼤量上市公司根本没有盈利，即便是盈利企业，市场也给出了极为

离谱的估值溢价 —— 信息技术板块的 12 个⽉远期市盈率⼀度接近 60 倍。
相⽐之下，当前主要科技公司普遍录得强劲盈利，估值溢价远不及当年极端。
更重要的是，互联⽹泡沫时期积累的估值泡沫花了数年才消散，⽽此轮科技股的估值虚⾼看起
来已经提前出清。
此外，⽀撑⼈⼯智能⾏情的基本⾯依然存在：超⼤规模云计算⼚商尚未下调强劲的资本⽀出计
划，算⼒需求依然旺盛，盈利增⻓保持强劲，宏观经济整体健康。
分析师提⽰：相似的叙事本⾝就是⻛险
尽管如此， TS Lombard 的 Perkins 仍提醒投资者保持警惕。
他指出，该机构⾃⾝⽀持⼈⼯智能扩张可持续性的论据，与 2000 年互联⽹泡沫破裂时市场上流
⾏的乐观叙事 " 惊⼈地相似 " 。
" 这⾄少应该让我们保持⼀份谦逊，"Perkins 说。
在他看来，历史上这⼀模式的演绎⽅式值得铭记：市场先经历⼀轮调整，随后迅速反弹，⽽真
正的崩跌往往在此之后才姗姗来迟。对于当前正在经历剧烈波动和深度亏损的投资者⽽⾔，这
⼀警⽰或许⽐任何技术指标都更值得认真对待。

## Document 036: 20261027_投資週報_AI資金壓力擾動市場，科技基本面仍穩健

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261027_投資週報_AI資金壓力擾動市場，科技基本面仍穩健.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 27 日
上週重點訊息
◼ AI 投資回報疑慮升溫，資金轉向債市避險：市場對大型科技企業 AI 基礎建設投資規模、融資
需求及未來獲利回收能力的疑慮升溫，導致科技股賣壓擴大，10 月 22 日 Nasdaq 指數下跌
約 2.6%。隨著市場風險偏好下降，避險資金轉向美國公債，10 年期公債殖利率單日下降約 19
個基點，創 2023 年 3 月以來最大單日跌幅，並於 23 日回升至 4.99%。市場關注焦點逐漸由
AI 投資規模轉向資本支出效益，後續大型科技企業財報及現金流表現，將成為評估 AI 產業評

價合理性的重要依據。
◼ 中東衝突推升通膨風險：中東衝突持續升溫，伊朗支持的葉門胡塞武裝攻擊沙烏地阿拉伯，引
發市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂，推升布蘭特原油價格於 10 月 23 日升至每桶 116.72 美元，單
週上漲約 6.2%，創 2022 年以來最高週收盤水準。能源價格攀升加劇全球通膨壓力，並壓抑
市場風險偏好。
◼ 法國政治動盪加劇財政疑慮，歐洲主權債風險重新受到關注：法國因生活成本上升爆發大規模
抗議，進一步加深市場對政府財政整頓能力的疑慮。10 月 19 日法國 10 年期公債殖利率上升
約 10 個基點至 4.88%附近，與德國公債利差擴大至約 144 個基點，創 2012 年以來最高收盤
水準，法股單日下跌約 1.5%，歐元同步走弱。市場擔憂政治不確定性可能削弱財政改革進度，
並透過主權債評價影響銀行業資產負債表。後續須關注法國財政政策、歐洲主權債利差及銀行
融資成本變化。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線宜審慎，中期聚焦基本面穩健的 AI 企業：近期 AI 資本支出、融資需求及評價調整

使科技股波動加劇，但先進製程與半導體設備需求仍具韌性，AI 中長期成長趨勢未改變。隨市
場焦點由成長題材轉向投資效益，企業營收與訂單能見度、自由現金流及資本效率的重要性提
升，具技術優勢、財務穩健及獲利能見度的企業相對有利，而高度依賴外部融資者宜審慎看待。
◼ 美債避險功能重現，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：市場風險偏好降溫時，美國公債仍具一定
避險效果。儘管 Fed 貨幣政策路徑與能源價格增添利率不確定性，短天期債券易受政策利率
牽動，超長天期債券面臨財政赤字、國債供給及期限溢價等壓力，相較之下，中天期美債的風
險報酬較為均衡。信用債方面，投資等級企業基本面及現金流仍具韌性，後續仍可持續關注。

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移

（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」

予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が 6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比 9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。

値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％）、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％）、アジア向けが前期

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

Lotes released its 3Q26 results on Nov 12th and held a results 
call on Nov 13th. Key takeaways from management include: 
(1) Lotes expects GM to begin recovering starting from 4Q26 along 
with the ramp up of AMD Venice CPU, with 4Q26 GM expected to 
reach similar levels as 2Q26 (GM was 44.8% in 3Q26, down from 
45.5% in 2Q26), and expects 4Q26 revenue to see low single digit 
QoQ growth. 
(2) Management is positive on the outlook for its server business in 
2027, expecting YoY growth to accelerate compared with 2026 
(server business with 57% YoY in 2026E per GSe), and expects 
server business to account for 60%+ of total 2027 revenue (vs. 54% 
in 2026E per GSe). The solid growth will be driven by (1) server unit 
growth of 10-15% in 2027 (up from ~10% in 2026), (2) ASP increase 
across new server CPU products (including server CPU sockets, PCIe 
cables and connectors), and (3) rising contribution from new 
AI-related products (including QD, SOCAMM and new NPO 
solutions). 
(3) The company is conservative on its 2027 GM outlook, indicating 
that overall GM is unlikely to reach the 50%+ level, mainly due to a 
higher contribution from margin-dilutive products (QD and 
SOCAMM) and delay in the ramp up of next gen server CPU products 
(MP timing for Intel Oak Stream has been delayed and will begin by 
late 1Q27 or early 2Q27). 
(4) Despite QD being margin dilutive (QD with 35-40% GM per GSe), 
management is positive that this product will drive revenue/EPS 
growth going forward. Management indicated that QD contributed 
~2% of 3Q26 revenue (with 150k+ pieces of monthly shipment per 
GSe vs. ~15k pieces of shipment in 2025). Management expects 
revenue contribution from QD will increase to 3-5% by December 
and further expand to ~10% in 2027. UTR for QD is currently full, 
and the company plans to double the capacity over the coming

### Page 3

quarters.
(5) The company expects NPO products to enter MP in 2027, although their revenue
contribution is likely to be <2%. Management anticipates revenue contribution to
increase by multiple folds in 2028, driven by broader adoption of 6.4T NPO products,
which are expected to have higher ASP than the mainstream 3.2T NPO products in 2027.
(6) For the CPU platform, Lotes mentioned Eagle Stream’s penetration is currently at
~50% (was 40-50% in 2Q26), while Birch Stream’s penetration is trending upward to

~50% (was ~40% in 2Q26). For AMD, SP5 remains mainstream while current penetration
for Venice is at <3%, and is expected to reach 3-5% by 2026 end. The company now
expects AMD Venice penetration to average 20% in 2027 (previously expect to reach
30-40% by 2027 end) while Intel’s Oak Stream is expected to reach an average
penetration of 10-15% in 2H27 (previously expect to reach 20-30% by 2027 end).
Investment view
We remain relatively cautious about Lotes’ near term business outlook, and we expect

limited room for meaningful margin improvement in the near term, mainly due to (1)
CPU socket shipments remain heavily focused toward legacy products, as the ramp up of
next gen CPU platforms (including AMD Venice and Intel Oak Stream CPU) continues to
face delays, and we believe the delays are partly due to potential shortages of key
components across the general server supply chain (including ABF substrate, memory,
PCB/CCL, etc), (2) growing contribution from margin-dilutive products starting from

2H26 (including QD and SOCAMM, which accounted for 4-5% of total 3Q26 revenue per
GSe), and (3) rising material cost could weigh on profitability as the company has limited
pricing power to fully pass on cost increases without risking market share.
However, we remain constructive on the outlook for general servers (we now expect
general server volume to increase by 20%/12%/8% YoY in 2026E-28E, was 14%/6%/6%
previously, see here), which we believe Lotes is the key beneficiary with its solid market

share in the server CPU socket market. We expect Lotes’ server revenue to see 38%
2026-28E CAGR (tracking above the 28% CAGR seen during the previous high-growth
server cycle in 2019-22), and we continue to believe server is the key margin expansion
driver given its above corporate average margin (general server socket GM at ~60% vs.
3Q26 total GM of 45%) and >55% revenue contribution throughout 2026E-28E.
Overall, we believe Lotes’ current share price has largely priced in the positive outlook

for general servers (the stock is trading at 16x+ 2027E P/E based on GSe/BBG
consensus, which is +2 STDV above the average P/E during the previous high growth
server cycle in 2019-22). The revenue contribution from higher margin new products
(including next gen CPU sockets, GPU sockets, and NPO products) is likely to remain
limited in the near-term.
We maintain our Neutral rating and lower our 12m TP to NT$1,640 (from NT1,670).
3Q26 overview
Lotes’ 3Q26 core business (OPI) was 23%/15% lower than GSe and BBG consensus, with

GM 1.6ppt/1.0ppt lower than GSe/BBG consensus, which was driven by higher material
costs and higher contribution from new AI-related products with lower margins. The
OPEX to sales ratio was 2.7ppt/2.4ppt higher than GSe and BBG consensus, due to
increasing R&D spending and labor costs. For earnings, Lotes’ NI was 18%/15% lower

### Page 5

Lotes is a connector supplier mainly focusing on the niche CPU socket market with more 
than 40% share in 2024, but we see pressure for its market share outlook in the coming 
years considering the intensifying competition in the server CPU market. We maintain 
our positive view on the company’s GM expansion potential longer term, which we 
believe will be mainly driven by better product mix (Venice server CPU to be released in 
2H26 and Oak Stream server CPU to be released in 2027, both with better ASP and 
margin). Meanwhile, Lotes’ CPU socket product line’s ASP/GM are higher than the 
company average, and we see scope for both to grow even faster in the new product 
generation given the larger socket size and pin count required. However, the company’s 
new product line, Quick Disconnects (UQD or MQD) for liquid cooling systems, is likely to 
deliver very limited revenue/gross profit contribution in the coming years (0.6/1.2% 
revenue and 0.4/0.8% GP contribution in 2026E/2027E) given thermal solution 
providers typically ship cold plates as integrated modules and leading thermal suppliers 
tend to secure QD supply in-house or through affiliated suppliers. We are Neutral rated 
on Lotes considering the share loss risk in server sockets and the shares trading at the 
historical peak P/E level, making valuation less attractive, despite the company’s solid 
revenue/GM expansion trend driven by socket upgrading demand. Key risks: (1) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected server shipment growth which would raise/lower 
server CPU socket demand; (2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs without 
product pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related 
contribution.

Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,640 is based on a target 
P/E of 16.0x (in line with the company’s average valuation since 2020) applied to our 
2027E EPS.  
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Stronger-/weaker-than-expected server CPU demand, 
(2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs, and (3) 
slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related contribution.

## Document 142: 261115_ms_pushback-on-cloud-semi-ole-memory-DG

Source PDF: all report pdf/261115_ms_pushback-on-cloud-semi-ole-memory-DG.pdf

Ethan Jia

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Our recent downgrades have generated investor pushback. We
acknowledge that demand remains strong, but believe exposure
should be reduced before cloud-capex deceleration becomes

visible.
Following Cloud Semis: Peak Growth, Less Upside  and Old Memory: Peak Pricing
Power?, we address the main investor questions below.
1. Are the cuts to Montage’s earnings forecasts justified?
We acknowledge that our revised 2027 RCD shipment assumptions may prove too
conservative. We would characterize the shipment and revenue revisions as
cautious, incorporating uncertainty around procurement timing, platform transitions
and supply-chain execution. These assumptions leave room for upside if customer

orders and deployment proceed more smoothly than we currently allow for.
Structural content growth remains supportive, and sustained order strength would
warrant revisiting the degree of conservatism in our estimates.
2. What justifies the multiple compression (across memory and cloud semis),
and where is the catalyst?
Our central consideration is to reduce exposure before the cloud-capex deceleration
becomes visible. Valuations can adjust as investors reassess the durability of

growth, even while earnings continue to rise. Peaking earnings-revision breadth and
weaker visibility into 2028 server production reduce our confidence in extrapolating
recent growth rates.
For old memory, the concern is also around the sustainability of pricing and returns
as shortages narrow. Limited fixed-price LTAs enabled rapid price increases during
the upcycle, but provide less protection when demand weakens. Our updated
supply-demand models point toward normalization into 2028, supporting lower

valuation multiples before peak profitability rolls over.
The potential catalysts are softer order momentum and slower price increases. Such
timing remains uncertain, but we believe waiting for these developments to become
fully visible risks missing the valuation adjustment. Faster deployment, renewed
earnings upgrades and sustained capex acceleration beyond 1Q27 would challenge
our cautious view. (Continued)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
November 15, 2026 03:36 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
 Idea
2

3. Have we changed our server or CPU shipment forecasts? If demand remains strong, 
why act now?
We have made no such industry-wide revisions. The hyperscalers’ 3Q results confirmed 
strong current spending. However, following the US midterms, our US team shifted 
approximately 5GW of expected US datacenter capacity additions from 2028 into 2029, 
equivalent to around 20% of previously expected 2028 additions (see Buy the Drop: AI 
Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms). Our US team's hyperscaler capex 
forecasts remained unchanged at US$1.6trn and US$1.8trn for 2027 and 2028. This 
creates greater uncertainty around the timing of equipment procurement and deployment 
even with spending intentions intact.
We believe the combination of the completed 3Q earnings season and greater post-
election execution uncertainty provides a reasonable point to incorporate more risk. 
Current demand can remain strong while the balance of potential earnings revisions and 
valuation outcomes becomes less attractive.
4. Why retain Overweight on CXMT and Nanya Tech?
We differentiate by product mix, technology migration and valuation. Niche memory has a 
smaller supply base, more volatile pricing and generally less direct exposure to AI 
demand. Its pricing can respond sharply to relatively modest changes in supply, customer 
inventories and consumer demand. These characteristics make recent scarcity-driven 
profitability more vulnerable as supply catches up.
CXMT and Nanya also face memory-cycle risk. However, CXMT has additional growth 
drivers from capacity expansion, localization and process tech migration. We also continue 
to see tight DDR5 supply, with CXMT management indicating that customers had reserved 
2027 capacity for relevant enterprise products. For Nanya, stronger process technology 
and a clearer DDR5 migration path differentiate it from other DDR4 suppliers in our 
coverage, with DDR5 already contributing approximately 10% of its revenue.
Aspeed and Montage have concentrated exposure to server deployment and the cloud 
investment cycle. Their content-growth opportunities remain intact, but their earnings 
trajectories and valuations are particularly sensitive to changes in deployment timing and 
expectations for cloud-spending growth. We therefore see a stronger case for reducing 
exposure ahead of visible deceleration.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 147: 261117_daiwa_andes

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_daiwa_andes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following on our results Memo for Andes, we widen our 
2026E net loss and maintain our Hold (3) rating. Management aims for a 
profitable 4Q26 to cover YTD losses, yet we are unsure how sustainable its 
bottom-line turnaround will be in the near term, given still uncertain timing 
of royalty conversion despite enriched IP and customer portfolios. That 
said, we remain structurally positive on Andes’s outlook, thanks to its 
leading position in the RISC-V architecture well prepared to capitalise on 
the AI cycle from cloud to edge to physical.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes in a mixed bag. We widen our 
2026E net loss for Andes to factor in its higher-than-expected opex YTD, 
but we keep our 2027E EPS largely unchanged to factor in its frugal opex 
control starting from 3Q26. Though bottom-line turnaround looks still 
bumpy in the near term on its suboptimal operating leverage due to still 
suboptimal scale, as a result of still uncertain timing of royalty revenue 
conversion, we merit management’s opex control to speed up bottom-line 
turnaround. This, together with its enriched RISC-V IP portfolio and 
broadened customer base for market-share gain, should help sustain its 
turnaround from 2H27 when topline expands to improve earnings quality 
and investor confidence.

Patience still for a sustainable turnaround. Despite this year’s business 
hiccups on delayed royalty ramp and opex volatility, we remain positive on 
Andes’s structural outlook, given its leading position in the RISC-V 
architecture offering enriched high-performance/low-power compute IP as 
substitutes to Arm and x86 CPU counterparts to capitalise on the secular AI 
cycle, with a broad coverage of applications from accelerated compute and 
embedded control to automotive. Management’s comments support our 
view, as it expects the RISC-V’s open-source ecosystem to drive innovation 
allowing designs of instruction sets tailored to customers’ specific needs, 
which should help enhance customer stickiness while attracting new 
customers for market-share gains. That said, patience may be still needed 
for Andes’s monetisation to bear fruit for a sustainable turnaround.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M 
TP to TWD240 (from TWD180), still based on DCF methodology (WACC: 
18%, terminal growth: 3%) which we believe is appropriate for a startup IP 
company such as Andes. Given the still bumpy road to any sustainable 
turnaround, we maintain our Hold (3) call with key upside/downside risks of 
any faster RISC-V ecosystem build/further delays of royalty ramp.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 29-48% above the consensus 
forecast likely due to our lower opex assumptions for Andes.

17 November 2026

Near-term turnaround still looks bumpy

➢ 2026E net loss likely to widen on opex volatility but … 
➢ … no harm to structural turnaround from 2H27 
➢ Maintaining our Hold (3) rating with a higher 12M TP of TWD240

Company profile 
Founded in 2005, Andes is an embedded microprocessor intellectual property (IP) provider with 
solutions in high-performance and low-power 32/64-bit processors and associated SoC platforms. Its 
solutions can be adopted for diverse applications such as IoT, 5G, AI, Bluetooth/WiFi devices, gaming 
and automotive. As a founding member of RISC-V International, Andes focuses on developing 
processor IPs based on the RISC-V instruction-set architecture while its mature V3 architecture is the 
main contributor of its royalty revenue.

### Page 5

5

Andes Technology (6533 TT): 17 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 17 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Andes’ Board of Directors consists of seven members, including three independent directors,
representing approximately 43% of the total board composition, exceeding the regulatory
minimum requirement of 20%. The roles of Chairman and President are held by separate
individuals, reflecting a clear division of leadership responsibilities.
Currently, the Board includes one female director among the seven seats. Andes acknowledges
that gender diversity at the board level remains an area for improvement and is actively
committed to strengthening diversity and inclusion as part of its ongoing governance
enhancement efforts.
Capital management 1
As a semiconductor IP provider without physical manufacturing operations, Andes does not
require significant capital expenditure for factories. Instead, its primary capital needs are related
to ongoing investment in research and development to support new IP development and capture
emerging market opportunities. From this perspective, we believe Andes is inclined to retain a
portion of its earnings to fund future IP innovation rather than fully distributing profits to
shareholders.
Andes did not distribute dividends prior to 2019. In 2019, it paid out 72% of its 2018 net profit as
dividends. Subsequently, the dividend payout ratio remained high at approximately 90% during
the period from 2020 to 2022. However, the payout ratio declined sharply to 21% in 2023 based
on 2022 net profit, reflecting a more conservative capital allocation approach.
Due to weakened profitability, Andes did not distribute dividends in 2024 or 2025, as earnings
were insufficient to support shareholder payouts. Management has prioritized financial flexibility
and the preservation of resources to navigate challenging market conditions and sustain long-
term R&D investment.
Related party &
transaction 1
Before July 2021, Andes conducted related party transactions with MediaTek (2454 TT) given
each company's chairman was the same person. MediaTek was Andes' largest customer with a
31% revenue contribution in 2013. Its revenue contribution has gradually declined in past years
to a single digit percentage in 2021. Since Andes' board of directors' election in July 2021, its
chairman and MediaTek's chairman are no longer the same person. Andes currently does not
have any meaningful related party transactions.
S Supply chain management 1
As a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, Andes mainly procures softwares &
services from its suppliers. Andes implements supplier management policies to ensure that there
is no violation of corporate social responsibility policy in the procurement contract with its
suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on-
site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment. It has set up a goal to reduce water
consumption by 2% every year through measures such as installing faucet water savers and
promoting the practice of saving water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1 Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on- site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment.
E GHG emissions 1
Andes is a semiconductor IP provider without any physical products. Thus, it does not have on-
site manufacturing activities to weigh on the environment. However, the company still applies
measures to reduce power consumption for its operations, such as turning off lights one hour per
day during the lunch break. It has set up a goal to reduce its total electricity consumption by 1-
2% every year.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

本次報告：法說會

虹堡 (5258 TT) 
2027 年營運重回成長

永豐觀點 
目前訂單能見度約一季，較先前有所改善。美洲客戶需求持續看增，預估明年成長
10%-20%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係考量(1) 新客戶訂單將逐漸貢獻營收，並持續精簡費用，
提升管理效率，2027 年獲利成長可期。(2) 主要營收區域歐洲及北美地區訂單狀
況皆轉好。評價上，以 2027 年稅後 EPS 5.04 元計算，目前本益比約 8 倍，明顯
低於過去五年本益比區間 10-17 倍，考量營運將重返成長，故給予 10 倍本益比
(原 11 倍)，維持買進的投資建議，下修目標價至 50 元。 
 近八季營收及 YoY 趨勢圖

### Page 2

2

全球前 15 大 POS 終端機製造業者：虹堡成立於 1993 年總部位於新北市新店區，
主要從事 POS 設備及相關產品之設計、開發與銷售，為台灣電子金融交易終端機
(POS)及 IC 晶片卡讀卡機製造商。主要營業項目為電子金融交易終端機(POS)及相
關產品之設計、開 發、銷售，這類終端機通過國際組織認證後經客戶在全球各地
通過當地收單機構驗證後即可用做當地商家收單使用。產品目前可支援各類型的支
付方式，包含非接觸卡片(contactless card)、晶片卡(chip card)、磁條卡(magnetic 
strip card)及新興的二維條碼及指紋辨識支付等。虹堡產品可概分為電子金融交易
終端機、個人金融應用產品、電子式收銀機及週邊設備等。國內主要競爭對手：振
樺電、樺漢、飛捷、伍豐等。 
記憶體價格飆漲，3Q26 獲利低於預期：在歐洲地區需求略有回穩下，虹堡 3Q26
營收 24.25 億元(+3.3%QoQ，+18.4%YoY)，受到記憶體價格上漲影響，毛利率
31.5%(+3pts QoQ，+4.6pts YoY)，因費用控管得宜，營利率 9.9%(+3.1pts QoQ，
+3.1pts YoY)，稅後淨利 1.5 億元(+25%QoQ，+8.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 1.36 元，
低於預期的 1.52 元。 
物料狀況預期轉好，4Q26 持續成長：虹堡 10 月營收 6.83 億元(-27.4%MoM，-
1.8%YoY)，衰退係受原物料價格上漲及法國動亂影響，持累積第四季達成率
27.1%，低於預期，然管理層預期上下半年營收比重 45:55，預估在零組件價格穩
定後，訂單將穩定出貨。研究處預估 4Q26 營收 25.16 億元(+3.4%QoQ，
+8.6%YoY)，考量零組件漲價未能為轉轉嫁給客戶，毛利率 31.8%(+0.3pts QoQ，
-3.5pts YoY)，營利率 10%(+0.1pts QoQ，-2.7pts YoY)，稅後淨利 2.12 億元
(+41.6%QoQ，-27.5%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 1.92 元。2026 年受記憶體價格上漲，
客戶下單轉為保守，預估 2026 年營收 89.1 億元(+13.3%YoY)，毛利率 30.9%(-
4pts YoY)，營業淨利 6.29 億元(-1%YoY)，稅後淨利 4.15 億元(-10.6%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 為 3.76 元。 
美洲地區客戶將開花結果，2027 年獲利重返成長：展望 2027 年，若以地區區分，
虹堡北美及南美客戶進展順利，許多專案已獲得客戶認證將開始出貨；歐洲地區，
法國新銀行訂單將持續出貨；亞太地區，因日本基期已高，2027 年預估微幅成長。
整體來說， 2027 年營收及獲利將重回成長的一年，然需關注記憶體價格對獲利及
訂單影響。研究部預估 2027 年營收 90.5 億元(+1.6%YoY)，毛利率 31.5%(+0.6pts 
YoY)，營業淨利 8.21 億元(+30.6%YoY)，稅後淨利 5.56 億元(+33.9%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 為 5.04 元。

## Document 216: [000270] 기아(000270) 예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향기아(000270) 예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향기아(000270) 예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향예상 대비 큰 PT_지역 믹스 악화 영향 - 기업.pdf

예상 대비 큰 PT/지역 믹스 악화 영향

▶Analyst 김성래 sr.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7751

Buy (유지) 
목표주가(하향): 210,000원

현재 주가(10/30) 95,600원 
상승여력 ▲119.7% 
시가총액 373,235억원

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아쉬웠습니다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추정치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 242: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd

### Page 1

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY)을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%)이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY)을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

## Document 279: Anthropic世纪IPO，创始人套现了130亿！

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic世纪IPO，创始人套现了130亿！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 以创纪录 IPO 募资逾千亿美元，七位联合创始⼈合计套现近 20 亿美元（约合⼈⺠
币 133.6 亿元），⾕歌、亚⻢逊等科技巨头合计持股约 28% 。然⽽， IPO 融资难以覆盖其庞
⼤的资本开⽀：公司已披露的基础设施承诺⾼达 1.05 万亿⾄ 1.08 万亿美元，未来仍将⾼度
依赖外部融资， AI 巨头的烧钱压⼒正浮出⽔⾯。
摘要：
Anthropic 以⼀场史⽆前例的 IPO 正式登陆公开市场，刷新科技公司上市融资纪录，同时也揭⽰
出这家 AI 巨头未来⾯临的天⽂数字级资本需求。
本次 IPO 定价每股 83 美元，⾼于此前 74 美元的区间中值， Anthropic 通过发⾏ A 类普通股及特殊 N
类股合计募资约 1048 亿美元，若承销商⾏使全部超额配售权，总额可达约 1170 亿美元。这⼀规
模远超 Facebook 2012 年 IPO 时创下的 160 亿美元纪录。
七位联合创始⼈在此次 IPO 中各⾃出售约 340 万股，按 83 美元定价计算，每⼈税前套现约 2.8 亿美

元，七⼈合计套现近 20 亿美元（约合⼈⺠币 133.6 亿元）。这⼀数字超出创始⼈此前按 74 美元中
值价格预设的约 2.5 亿美元⽬标，每⼈额外进账约 3000 万美元。
创始⼈套现逾 20 亿美元，慈善承诺存弹性空间
七位创始⼈的套现规模引发市场关注。 Anthropic 在 IPO ⽂件中披露，每位创始⼈已承诺将其个
⼈持股的 80% ⽤于慈善事业，以此回应外界对创始⼈⼤规模变现的质疑。
然⽽，该承诺的约束⼒存在明显局限。公司在⽂件中明确指出，上述承诺属于 " ⾃愿、可撤销的
慈善意向声明，不构成具有法律约束⼒的义务 " ，且若创始⼈认为有必要，可缩减慈善捐赠⽐
例。
科技巨头争相⼊股，亚⻢逊与⾕歌合计持股 28%
除公开发⾏的 A 类普通股外， Anthropic 还向多家科技巨头定向发售了总计 260 亿美元的 N 类⽆投
票权股份，吸引了⾏业内最具影响⼒的战略投资者。
其中，英伟达与⾕歌各认购 100 亿美元 N 类股份， AMD 、博通、美光、微软及台积电⽠分剩余份
额。

在整体股权结构上，⾕歌持有 Anthropic 约 13.4% 的股份；亚⻢逊则通过将此前投资时获得的可
转换票据部分转股，持股⽐例达 14.6%—— 若亚⻢逊将全部票据转换为股权，持股⽐例最⾼可接
近 24% 。两⼤云计算巨头合计持有 Anthropic 约 28% 的股份。
逾万亿美元基础设施承诺，资本需求远超融资规模
尽管本次 IPO 融资规模创下历史纪录， Anthropic ⾯临的资本压⼒依然巨⼤。
根据截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的季度数据， Anthropic 未来 12 个⽉内仅算⼒与基础设施⽀出承诺就⾼
达 707 亿美元；⾄ 2030 年底前，这⼀数字将攀升⾄ 4516 亿美元。此外，公司还积累了数千亿美
元的其他或有承诺及尚未定价的⽀出项⽬。

## Document 307: Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-22).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
09:49 November 13 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest
reading since late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of severe economic stress.
Renewed cost-of-living and interest rate pressures were front and centre following another lift in

inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage
holders are feeling the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group falling to a record low.

### Page 2

Consumers have become noticeably more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the relevant
sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge
up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business survey showed confidence slipped further
to –15 in October as conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic. Of concern for the
outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow materially faster than selling prices, further
compressing already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter financial conditions can jolt activity. The
value of new dwelling finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly decline in the series’
history, taking approvals 25% lower year-to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined with the housing tax changes announced in May.

Owner-occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home buyers proving particularly sensitive
to the deterioration in affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum across the domestic economy,
consistent with Westpac’s expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the

consensus view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the result, but core inflation was
also marginally stronger than forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the past four
months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost
inflation through. Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace, gaining 0.3% in
October.
The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor

Waller prior to the release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and the labour market while
showing significant concern over the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the passthrough
of energy and technology input inflation. His conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the
monthly rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward supporting an increase in our policy
rate of 25 basis points at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI inflation data out

later today are similar to the past two monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent or
even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also kept the focus on inflation risks and
stated or implied the FOMC needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s updates,
pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by
September. We believe this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core inflation ex

shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-month annualised basis and the labour market is
balanced; but it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate, US households are under pressure from
the cost of living, and their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing costs. Highlighting

### Page 3

Related articles
this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and 2010. Our November Market Outlook will
provide a full update on our expectations for the US and the world when released on Westpac IQ this
afternoon.

## Document 391: Productivity_ reasons for optimism (2026-11-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Productivity_ reasons for optimism (2026-11-22).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#gdp #australia #employment #housing #rba #productivity
Productivity: reasons for optimism
By Pat Bustamante
Senior Economist, Westpac
08:37 November 17 2026 Share
The supply-side outlook is improving as capital deepening strengthens, reallocation drags
fade and AI diffusion gathers pace.
The supply-side outlook is looking brighter. A pickup in capital deepening and a fading ‘reallocation’
drag are already evident in the Annual National Accounts, with AI diffusion offering further upside.

This gives us greater conviction that productivity growth can return towards 1% or more over time,

### Page 2

Growth in 2024-25 was a little firmer than first thought
The 2025–26 figures show somewhat firmer economic activity and business investment than previously
estimated, particularly in 2024–25. The economy grew by 2.3% in year-average terms, broadly in line
with the initial quarterly National Accounts estimate of 2.4%. However, growth in 2024–25 was revised
up by 0.3ppts to 1.6%, largely reflecting stronger private business investment. Business investment is

now estimated to have increased by 1.1% in 2024–25, compared with the initial estimate of a 0.2%
decline. The revisions do not materially alter the broader productivity story, but they do suggest that the
economy was growing a little more strongly for the same rate of inflation in 2025.
above the RBA’s assumption and closer to Treasury’s. The recovery illustrates why it is important to
focus on longer-term trends in productivity growth rather than on the latest year or two, which can be

volatile.
Capital deepening is returning. Investment is picking up and, outside mining, market-sector capital
per hour worked is now at a record high, rising a strong 1.5% in 2025–26.
The reallocation headwind is easing. The shift of labour towards non-market industries, which
generally have lower measured productivity levels, has reduced the level of aggregate productivity by
around 1ppt over the past 15 years. With the expansion of the non-market sector plateauing, this

drag looks set to fade.
Productivity growth is about more than capital. Multifactor productivity, which captures how efficiently
businesses combine labour and capital, was the dominant driver of market-sector productivity growth
in 2025–26. Meanwhile, stronger investment in IT, utilities and professional services should provide a
further lift as new capital is put to work.

### Page 3

The investment upswing is broadening
The Annuals also provide a richer picture of the supply side, including investment by industry and the
evolution of the capital stock, details that are not available in the quarterly accounts. These data point
to a notable lift in investment in the market (ex-mining) part of the economy, where investment has
gone from around 10% of nominal GDP in to just over 11.5%. Outside of this, investment has been
softer, particularly in the mining sector.

### Page 4

Within the market sector, investment by the IT industry, increasingly focused on data centres,
has risen to its highest share of nominal GDP since the technology boom of the 1990s. Utilities
investment, including renewables and associated infrastructure, is stronger still, at its highest share
since 1983, while investment by the professional services industry has reached a record high.

### Page 5

Across the non-market sector (including health, education and public administration), investment has
also held at a higher share of the economy, driven by the health and public administration (including
defence) industries.

### Page 6

Investment is translating into capital deepening
The investment upswing is also beginning to translate into capital deepening as industries look to
rebuild capital stocks after a period of soft growth. Outside mining, market-sector capital per hour
worked is at a record high and well above its pre-pandemic level. That is an encouraging
development, particularly given that the sector accounts for 70% of total employment.
There are important divergences beneath the aggregate. Mining continues to experience capital

shallowing, as investment has been insufficient to cover wear and tear and capital requirements
associated with additional workers. By contrast, the capital stock in the non-market sector appears to
have caught up with the surge in employment we have seen since the pandemic, with capital per
worker increasing for the first time since the pandemic.

### Page 7

A major productivity headwind is starting to fade
Capital deepening will help support productivity growth across the economy. The Annuals show that
productivity in the market sector (excluding mining) continues to grow by around 1% in year-average
terms, while mining remains a drag on aggregate productivity (down 0.8% in 2025–26 and almost 30%
since 2017).

### Page 8

At the same time, a major recent headwind is easing as the rapid expansion of the non-market sector
approaches slows. This matters for aggregate productivity. Productivity growth reflects both gains
within industries, through capital deepening, technology and better processes, and the ‘reallocation’ of
resources between industries with different productivity levels.
Measured productivity in the non-market sector is considerably lower than across much of the market

economy, partly reflecting measurement issues (output in these sectors is generally not well measured,
particularly when there are quality changes). As employment shifted towards these industries, this
compositional effect weighed on aggregate productivity. Our estimates suggest it has reduced the
level of aggregate productivity by a significant 1ppt over the past 15 years and almost 0.3ppts
over the three years to 2025–26.

### Page 9

Productivity gains were about more than capital
It wasn’t only capital deepening that drove productivity growth. Multifactor productivity (MFP), which
captures how efficiently businesses combine labour and capital through changes in processes,
technology, know-how, was the dominant driver of market-sector productivity growth this year. This
gives us more conviction that the supply side is normalising, and productivity growth will improve from
its recent lows.

### Page 10

Where productivity is improving, and where it isn’t
At the industry level, some of those recording stronger investment are also beginning to deliver
stronger productivity outcomes, although the picture remains uneven.
IT stands out at the positive end of the spectrum, alongside improving outcomes in manufacturing,
administrative and support services, and transport.. These are also industries where the diffusion of
AI and other digital technologies could deliver further productivity gains. Utilities and

professional services should also benefit as stronger investment translates into greater capital intensity
and efficiency.
At the other end, mining remains a major drag, consistent with the capital shallowing evident in the
investment and capital-stock data. Construction is another notable laggard, with productivity continuing
to deteriorate despite strong demand from housing, population growth, renewable-energy infrastructure
and data-centre projects. Construction productivity fell around 2% in 2025–26 and almost 21% over the

13 years from 2012–13 to 2025–26.

### Page 11

A more constructive supply-side story
The Annuals provide a more constructive supply-side picture than the aggregate productivity figures
alone suggest. Market-sector productivity outside mining continues to advance, the reallocation drag
from the expanding non-market sector looks likely to fade, and capital deepening is strengthening
across important parts of the economy. The strong MFP outcome in the market sector gives us more
conviction that the supply side is normalising, and productivity growth will improve going ahead.

The critical question is whether the strong investment underway in technology, professional services,
electricity and related infrastructure translates into stronger productivity growth as that new capital is
put to work. If it does, the improvement in capital intensity evident in the Annuals could mark the
beginning of a more durable lift in Australia’s productivity performance.

### Page 12

## Document 423: Top of Mind - Higher Bond Yields_ How Concerning_

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the r ails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, pr ovided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
